# Social Studies Teacher Starter Pack — Primary 1–3

**Curriculum:** Thai Basic Education Core Curriculum B.E. 2551 (2008), revised indicators where applicable
**Coverage:** 104 official indicator lessons
**Source alignment:** Each lesson retains the indicator code, learning standard and original Thai indicator wording from the curriculum database.

> Classification labels are shown as currently stored. P1–P3 include verified OBEC between-learning / terminal classifications.

## Teaching guidance

Use the indicator as the learning target. Adapt examples to the learners' community and grade level. For religious content, respect the learner's faith and teach the curriculum's comparative language inclusively. For history and civics, distinguish evidence from opinion. For geography, use maps, local observations and reliable data. For economics, use familiar household and community examples.

## Indicator lessons

### 1. SO1.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P1-01` — บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO1.1-P1-01 (บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO1.1-P1-01 matters in religion, ethics and ways of life: “บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO1.1-P1-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Draw a three-picture story showing a respectful way to use a teaching, value or practice connected to “บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป” at home or school. Tell one reason for the action. **Answer:** The response accurately applies the relevant teaching/value/practice, gives a respectful action and justifies it. Never assess personal belief or require worship.

### 7. SO1.2-P1-03

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P1-03` — ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

### 8. SO2.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P1-01` — บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO2.1-P1-01 (บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO2.1-P1-01 matters in civics, rights and responsibilities: “บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO2.1-P1-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Draw a class or community situation connected to “บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน”. Label who is involved, show one fair rule or responsibility, and tell what each person should do. **Answer:** The response identifies affected people, applies the relevant right/duty/rule/process, and justifies a fair and feasible action.

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P1-01` — ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO3.1-P1-01 (ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO3.1-P1-01 matters in economics and resource decisions: “ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO3.1-P1-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Use a simple picture-choice card about “ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Choose a sensible action and tell why it makes responsible use of the relevant resource or opportunity. **Answer:** The response correctly applies the economic idea, compares relevant choices/resources/trade-offs and justifies a responsible decision.

### 17. SO4.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P1-01` — บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO4.1-P1-01 (บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO4.1-P1-01 matters in history and historical evidence: “บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO4.1-P1-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Arrange three picture cards, events or source details connected to “บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน” in order. Tell or draw one thing that happened or changed. **Answer:** The response uses accurate chronological/source evidence and supports a defensible historical explanation; distinguish evidence from opinion.

- **Official indicator:** บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P1-01` — บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

### 25. SO5.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P1-01` — จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO5.1-P1-01 (จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO5.1-P1-01 matters in geography and human–environment relationships: “จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO5.1-P1-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Draw or label a simple map or picture connected to “จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. Mark the place or feature and show one suitable way people can care for or respond to it. **Answer:** The response accurately interprets the relevant map/data/environmental relationship and supports a feasible action with evidence.

- **Official indicator:** ใช้แผนผังแสดงตำแหน่งของสิ่งต่าง ๆ ในห้องเรียน

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P1-03` — ใช้แผนผังแสดงตำแหน่งของสิ่งต่าง ๆ ในห้องเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

### 32. SO1.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P2-01` — บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO1.1-P2-01 (บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO1.1-P2-01 matters in religion, ethics and ways of life: “บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO1.1-P2-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Draw a three-picture story showing a respectful way to use a teaching, value or practice connected to “บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ” at home or school. Tell one reason for the action. **Answer:** The response accurately applies the relevant teaching/value/practice, gives a respectful action and justifies it. Never assess personal belief or require worship.

### 34. SO1.1-P2-03

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P2-03` — ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.

3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

### 39. SO1.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P2-01` — ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

### 41. SO2.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P2-01` — ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO2.1-P2-01 (ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.
2. **Explain / analyse:** Explain how or why the idea in SO2.1-P2-01 matters in civics, rights and responsibilities: “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO2.1-P2-01 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Draw a class or community situation connected to “ปฏิบัติตนตามข้อตกลง กติกา กฎ ระเบียบและหน้าที่ ที่ต้องปฏิบัติในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Label who is involved, show one fair rule or responsibility, and tell what each person should do. **Answer:** The response identifies affected people, applies the relevant right/duty/rule/process, and justifies a fair and feasible action.

### 46. SO2.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO2.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO2.2-P2-02` — ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุผู้มีบทบาท อำนาจในการตัดสินใจในโรงเรียนและชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.2-P2-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

### 47. SO3.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P2-01` — ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO3.1-P2-01 (ระบุทรัพยากรที่นำมาผลิตสินค้าและบริการที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P2-01` — ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ใช้คำระบุเวลาที่แสดงเหตุการณ์ในอดีต ปัจจุบัน และอนาคต

### 59. SO5.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P2-01` — ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุสิ่งแวดล้อมทางธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น ซึ่งปรากฏระหว่างบ้านกับโรงเรียน

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

### 76. SO2.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P3-01` — สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO2.1-P3-01 (สรุปประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนตามประเพณีและวัฒนธรรมในครอบครัวและท้องถิ่น); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.

### 77. SO2.1-P3-02

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกพฤติกรรมการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองและผู้อื่นที่อยู่ในกระแสวัฒนธรรมที่หลากหลาย

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P3-02` — บอกพฤติกรรมการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองและผู้อื่นที่อยู่ในกระแสวัฒนธรรมที่หลากหลาย
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกพฤติกรรมการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองและผู้อื่นที่อยู่ในกระแสวัฒนธรรมที่หลากหลาย

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P3-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

### 83. SO3.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P3-01` — จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: จำแนกความต้องการและความจำเป็นในการใช้สินค้าและบริการในการดำรงชีวิต

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

### 89. SO4.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P3-01` — เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เทียบศักราชที่สำคัญตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P3-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P3-02` — แสดงลำดับเหตุการณ์สำคัญของโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยระบุหลักฐานและแหล่งข้อมูลที่เกี่ยวข้อง

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

### 96. SO4.3-P3-03

- **Standard:** SO4.3
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** เล่าวีรกรรมของบรรพบุรุษไทยที่มีส่วนปกป้องประเทศชาติ

**Objective:** `SO4.3-P3-03` — เล่าวีรกรรมของบรรพบุรุษไทยที่มีส่วนปกป้องประเทศชาติ
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เล่าวีรกรรมของบรรพบุรุษไทยที่มีส่วนปกป้องประเทศชาติ

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.3-P3-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

### 97. SO5.1-P3-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P3-01` — สำรวจข้อมูลทางภูมิศาสตร์ในโรงเรียนและชุมชน โดยใช้แผนผัง แผนที่ และรูปถ่าย เพื่อแสดงความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ ทิศทาง
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

Mark the place or feature and show one suitable way people can care for or respond to it. **Answer:** The response accurately interprets the relevant map/data/environmental relationship and supports a feasible action with evidence.
### 102. SO5.2-P3-04

- **Standard:** SO5.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายความแตกต่างของลักษณะเมืองและชนบท

**Objective:** `SO5.2-P3-04` — อธิบายความแตกต่างของลักษณะเมืองและชนบท
- **Learning intention:** Learners demonstrate the knowledge or performance stated in this indicator.

**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายความแตกต่างของลักษณะเมืองและชนบท

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.2-P3-04: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Independent practice**
1. **Recall / identify:** From the lesson's picture, short source, map or classroom example, identify 2 accurate facts, terms, people, practices or features needed for this indicator: “อธิบายความแตกต่างของลักษณะเมืองและชนบท”. **Answer:** Identify 2 accurate items directly required by SO5.2-P3-04 (อธิบายความแตกต่างของลักษณะเมืองและชนบท); accept equivalent wording and relevant examples, not unrelated facts.

Support the explanation with one relevant Thai or local example. **Answer:** Give an accurate explanation of the idea or relationship in SO5.2-P3-04 and link it to one relevant example. Accept other accurate examples.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Draw or label a simple map or picture connected to “อธิบายความแตกต่างของลักษณะเมืองและชนบท”.
